HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001501/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_11cffd5a65531d48dc43","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:6:3\",\"root_001501\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Unutmama vaadi, yalnız zihinsel kaybı değil, emanet edilen sözü terk edip işlemez bırakmayı da dışlar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001501/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_214bb3d3d0b7557b5ffc","text":"87:9–10 confirmed that the gathered recitation does not stop with the addressee but becomes reminder and recollection in another. 87:11 then corrected `فَلَا تَنسَىٰ` from a purely cognitive promise: its `ن س ي/B002` abandonment sense stands against another person’s active decision to move the reminder aside.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001501/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_38a5c745237a517b26ae","text":"2. **Protection from becoming a cast-off thing.** Lexical evidence: ن س ي B003 (forgotten discarded low-value thing), غ ث و B004 in 87:5 (unregarded refuse), ب ق ي B001 in 87:17 (persistence). Structural cue: the chaff image sits immediately before the anti-forgetting promise. Assigned role: نسيان threatens to turn revelation into abandoned residue; recitation keeps it from that status. Abductive move: cognitive abandonment and material refuse are structurally analogous. Reading change: \"you will not forget\" becomes preservation from degradation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3e1bfa36434b291ca885","text":"4. **Teaching by collected cycles.** Lexical evidence: قرء B002 appointed time/cycle and B005 appointed time arriving; قدر B001 measured term; يسر B001 ease in 87:8. Structural cue: divine instruction is followed by divine easing. Assigned role: recitation unfolds in timed, measured pedagogy. Abductive move: the packet supplies cycle/time and measure; I infer paced instruction. Reading change: the promise becomes an ordered curriculum rather than a single transfer.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6bfeaec17a2fba1618b2","text":"2. **Womb-like retention of revelation.** Lexical evidence: قرء B003 womb gathering/bearing and B004 womb enclosing pregnancy; رحم B003 womb in 87:0; خلق B002 originating in 87:2. Structural cue: the opening mercy/womb field precedes \"we will make you recite.\" Assigned role: the addressee's memory becomes a vessel that encloses what is being brought forth. Abductive move: the packet supplies womb branches for both رحم and قرء; I infer an analogy between gestation and retained recitation. Reading change: revelation is carried until uttered, not simply heard and repeated.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001210/B001","root_001211/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_6c68a3240fbfff50ef0d","text":"1. **Recitation is gathered speech made retainable.** The “gathering speech into recitation” variant of `ق ر ء/B001` anchors `سَنُقْرِئُكَ`, and `ن س ي/B001` supplies loss of recall. The causative first-person verb followed by `فَلَا` gives a production-to-retention sequence; later `ذ ك ر` repetition in 87:9–10 confirms recall as the receiving mechanism. Recitation’s role is to assemble discourse into a whole, while the negation protects that whole from dispersal. I infer that gathering enables retention. The ayah reads as formation of durable verbal memory, not only an instruction to read.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_98ea7e7cb6a4b243c407","text":"3. **Against abandonment, not just mental lapse.** Lexical evidence: نسي B002 leaving/abandoning a covenant or command; رعي B006 preserving a claim; ذكر B009 reminder/admonition in 87:9. Structural cue: reminder follows the anti-forgetting promise. Assigned role: \"forgetting\" can mean neglect of an entrusted obligation. Abductive move: the packet gives abandonment and later reminder; I infer that preservation is ethical as well as cognitive. Reading change: \"you will not forget\" becomes \"you will not be left to abandon the entrusted recitation.\"","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001501/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9a91a0d9170b44dafa6f","text":"2. **The negation also excludes abandonment.** `ن س ي/B002` is leaving or neglecting, not merely involuntary forgetting. The window places discarded `غُثَاء` behind the clause and active `يَتَجَنَّبُهَا` in 87:11 ahead of it. Those two forms distinguish being cast off, being preserved, and deliberately moving aside. The branch assigns `فَلَا تَنسَىٰ` covenantal or practical continuity: the recited matter will not be left unused. I infer agency-sensitive contrast from the sequence. The reading changes from memory assurance alone to protection against neglect.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001210/B005","root_001211/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ae95c38419c8cb77db84","text":"3. **The future can be heard as staged recurrence.** The appointed-time or cycle variant of `ق ر ء/B005` is activated by the future prefix `سـ`, by the earlier measured lifecycle, and by repeated reminder in 87:9–10. Its role is to distribute recitation through successive occasions rather than compress it into one event. I infer periodic delivery from those cues; the form itself primarily supports causative recitation, so this reading is exploratory. On this reading, non-forgetting is continuity across renewed episodes of reception.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001210/B001","root_001211/B001","root_001501/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b7544085f9e6bfe7fa61","text":"1. **Recitation as ordered gathering against loss.** Lexical evidence: ق ر ء B001/B002 (gathering speech into recitation; teaching recitation), ن س ي B001 (loss of recall). Structural cue: 87:5's disordered refuse precedes 87:6's gathered speech. Assigned role: نقرئك gathers the discourse into a repeatable whole; فلا تنسى blocks its decay. Abductive move: memory is preserved when speech is structured as recitation. Reading change: the promise is active formation of durable order, not merely a forecast that forgetting will not occur.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B004","root_001210/B002","root_001211/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d2932fce42fe9e2a40e2","text":"3. **Teaching as preparation for public reminder.** Lexical evidence: ق ر ء B002/B006 (reading/teaching; devout reader), ذ ك ر B004/B009 in 87:9 and ذ ك ر B004 in 87:15. Structural cue: \"We will make you recite\" precedes \"so remind.\" Assigned role: divine recitation installs the material that can later be made public as reminder. Abductive move: one cannot remind beneficially unless first made a reliable bearer. Reading change: 87:6 becomes an authorization and training scene.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d97c42a55586fc91d820","text":"87:9-10 made this ayah less private: the preserved recitation exists so reminder can happen and the fearful can remember. 87:18-19 made retention look like a living counterpart to written sheets: oral preservation and archival صحف mirror each other.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_f42663366db252ff727f","text":"1. **Recitation as gathered speech secured against loss.** Lexical evidence: قرء B001 gathering speech into recitation and B002 reading/teaching recitation; نسي B001 forgetting/loss of recall. Structural cue: future divine \"we will make you recite\" followed by \"so you do not forget.\" Assigned role: قرء gathers the material; نسي names the failure being blocked. Abductive move: the packet supplies gathering and forgetting; I infer that divine recitation creates retention.
